Palantir Technologies (PLTR) achieved a Rule of 40 score of 158% in Q3 2026, up from 81% in Q4 2024, driven by 96% year-over-year revenue growth and a 62% adjusted operating margin. U.S. commercial revenues surged 148% to $984 million while government revenues climbed 96%, supported by expanding AI Platform adoption. The company generated $1.22 billion in free cash flow with a 53% margin, though elevated investor expectations and valuation multiples present risks.
<<<INSIGHTS>>>
PLTR | positive | Exceptional Rule of 40 score of 158%, accelerating revenue growth of 96% YoY, strong profitability with 62% adjusted operating margin, and robust free cash flow generation of $1.22 billion demonstrate outstanding operational performance and business momentum.
SNOW | neutral | Reported solid 35% revenue growth supported by AI Data Cloud adoption and expanding enterprise relationships, but performance is notably lower than PLTR's 96% growth rate, positioning it as a comparable but less impressive peer.
DDOG | neutral | Delivered 35% revenue growth and 28% non-GAAP operating margin with improving profitability, but similar to Snowflake, its growth rate significantly lags PLTR's performance, making it a solid but less exceptional alternative in the AI software space.
<<<END>>>
